# Third-party licences

Generated by `python3 tools/licenses.py`; do not edit by hand. Lists every
third-party dependency in the Rust lockfiles (`tools/Cargo.lock`,
`tools/refactor/rsscan/Cargo.lock`), with the licence text that ships in each package.
Alto's own licence is in `LICENSE`; component origins are in `NOTICE`.
